### GC-57 — Companion Training

Source identity: `companion_training`. Original prerequisite: **Animal Companion feat or proficiency in Animal Handling**.

**Original effect, unchanged**

Beast companions you command gain +2 to attack and damage rolls and inherit your Survival proficiency for tracking. As a bonus action, command one companion to attack, dash, disengage, or help; at level 10+, you may issue the same command to two companions that can hear you. When a companion reduces a foe to 0 HP, you gain advantage on your next attack against a creature within 30 feet before the end of your next turn.

**Proposed complete procedure**

Require the exact Animal Companion feat OR actual Animal Handling proficiency. Your genuinely bound/tamed Beast companions under your lawful current command gain+2 to their attack rolls and+2flat once per native attack damage roll, not per die/type or critical doubling. NEW no benefit to an ordinary allied Beast, non-Beast construct/undead/familiar, or a body you have no actual companion/control relation with. Original source-owner stats remain; add this feat layer once across overlapping trainers. For real tracking checks, if you are proficient in Survival, the companion uses its ownWIS plus your currentPB as the inherited proficiency contribution; expertise is not inherited, and a better real own contribution can replace it, not add twice. If you lack Survival proficiency, nothing is inherited. During your regular turn spend the sharedBAcommand within60ft to one companion that can hear you, selecting attack, Dash, Disengage or Help. At total level10+, the same command can name two audible owned companions taking that same action in their attached phases. This changes command recipient count only: one shared offensive companion action per owner numbered round remains. For attack, designate at most one to spend that offense; the other may move defensively and Dodge, not bank an attack. Two pure support actions can both resolve if legal. Command fails without refund if eligibility is lost; no extra independent turns/Reactions. When one of your actually commanded Beast companions reduces a meaningful hostile from positiveHP to0, you gain one advantage entitlement for your next attack against a creature within30ft of you before your next future regular end. NEW only an attack meeting30ft consumes it; a farther attack does not. Same-family reapplication refreshes one entitlement/deadline, never queues multiple. Death is not required, source floors/copy/no-reward restrictions apply.

**Explicit NEW choices:** Actual Beast companion relation, additive single layer, inherited proficiency arithmetic, two-recipient command within unchanged shared offense and bounded owner reward.

**Worked adjudications**

- At level10, two companions can each perform a legal Help from the one shared command; an Attack command still permits only one shared offensive action.

- A commanded Beast downs a hostile: the owner's next attack within30ft gains advantage before the deadline; no extra attack is granted.

**Dependencies:** Current Core/combat/conditions; actual equipment and feat acquisition; shared proposed feat conventions.

### GC-58 — Primal Instinct

Source identity: `primal_instinct`. Original prerequisite: **Wisdom 13+, proficiency in Survival**.

**Original effect, unchanged**

You have advantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks relying on hearing or smell. You cannot be surprised while conscious. You can track creatures across difficult terrain without penalty. Under moonlight or while transformed, you gain +2 to initiative and once per short rest you may howl or roar as a free action: allies within 30 feet who can hear you gain advantage on their next attack against a creature you are tracking or have damaged this encounter.

**Proposed complete procedure**

Gain advantage on actualWIS Perception checks relying on working hearing or smell, without a passive±5 adjustment. While conscious, surprise restrictions are suppressed as in Alert, with no retroactive actions. When actually tracking a real trail, ignore penalties caused solely by difficult terrain; preserve absent evidence, concealment, age, weather and source-specific no-trace restrictions. NEW the moonlight/transformed clause qualifies both the+2initiative and howl: at the actual initiative roll, if physically illuminated by real existing moonlight or in an actual body-transforming form, add2. It does not establish an astronomical calendar, count a moon-themed spell as moonlight, or treat an illusion/disguise as body transformation. Once perSR while that predicate holds, on your own regular turn without an Action cost, audibly howl/roar and choose one actual creature you are currently tracking through a legal source or personally damaged during this encounter. Other allies within30ft who hear at the moment gain advantage on their next attack against that chosen creature before your next future regular end. NEW recipient list and target are fixed, no continuing range tether and no extra attack; one family entitlement per ally, consumed by its first eligible attack hit/miss/cancellation. The body must actually be able to howl or roar; articulated speech or a shared language is not required. Silence or an anatomy unable to make that sound prevents it. Conditions otherwise permit this brief free communication but do not grantActions. Tracking alone does not reveal a target's present location for an attack.

**Explicit NEW choices:** Actual hearing/smell, terrain-only tracking relief, literal moonlight/body-change predicate and complete free-own-turnSR howl contract.

**Worked adjudications**

- A transformed owner howls on its turn for a foe it actually damaged; hearing allies receive one next-attack advantage through the owner's next regular end.

- A moon-painted lantern is not actual moonlight. The feat creates neither transformation access nor current location knowledge from an old trail.

**Dependencies:** Current Core/combat/conditions; actual equipment and feat acquisition; shared proposed feat conventions.

## NEW animal companion profiles

These three finite feat-only profiles require an actual eligible animal. They do not import monster traits, supply a free animal or replace Ranger class cards.

### profile

AC12+floor(current ownerPB/2); dynamic maximumHP5+current total owner level; all ordinary saves use current ownerPB alone; other ability checks use template ability modifiers, no PB unless a real source grants it. Template INT3 is a feat-profile value, not proof that a real source beast meets the INT<7 admission gate.

### nativeBody

Preserve actual original source profile, body identity, current health ratio, size, breathing, conditions, disease, hunger, custody and age. Templates replace only their stated combat/ability/speed values for this bond. Any actual anatomy incapacity/sensory blocker remains. No template creates a supernatural sense, trait, hand or extra body.

### actions

One command and offensive allowance across owner companions/devices per numbered round. Commanded body has one attached phase after whole owner regular turn, move plus chosen legal attack or standard Dash/Disengage/Help/Dodge; Dash adds one currentSpeed to its phase cap, Disengage suppresses opportunities for that phase, Help follows actual5ft/physically possible aid and expires at the next original companion-start marker. No independent BA/Reaction/turn/death save; no extra-turn phase. Uncommanded only defensive movement/Dodge.

### conversion

Admission requires a real living animal with positiveHP and positive maximumHP and an actual eligible willing/tamed receipt. Initial and release conversion preserves health ratio using max(1,floor(newMax*oldHP/oldMax)), not a heal/damage event. At0 the feat animal is dead, never eligible for conversion. Save the prior source profile rather than deriving a new one on release.

### recovery

No resummon, free rest healing, unprinted Hit Dice or new original animal from a saved profile. External legal healing/resurrection retains its actual costs and rules; resurrection returns the same body unbonded, and new bonding still requires the post-death completedLR plus full procedure. Capacity change never repairs injury, replenishes resources or creates death/HP-loss triggers.

### Ground companion

**id:** feat-animal.ground

**status:** NEW feat-only profile, not a recovered monster record

**eligibleBody:** An actual positive-HP living Small or Medium terrestrial Beast with ordinary biting anatomy; preserve its actual size. Dog/fox-like examples are illustrative, not free supplied animals.

**abilities:** {"STR": 12, "DEX": 12, "CON": 12, "INT": 3, "WIS": 12, "CHA": 6}

**movement:** {"walkFeet": 30}

**attack:** One melee bite, reach5ft, attack=ownerPB+owner actualWIS modifier, damage1d6piercing; no addedWIS damage and no automatic level scaling.

**sense:** Choose its actually sourced keen hearing OR smell, if present, for advantage on that sense's actual Perception checks. No new keen sense if absent.

**limits:** No Pack Tactics, knockdown, grapple, multiattack, burrow or extraordinary source powers. Carrying uses this actual body/STR and ordinary Load/pounds limits, real harness/hands. A mount must separately meet real riding eligibility.

### Flying companion

**id:** feat-animal.flyer

**status:** NEW feat-only profile, not a recovered monster record

**eligibleBody:** An actual positive-HP living Tiny winged Beast that already possesses natural flight and a pecking/biting attack. No Hover.

**abilities:** {"STR": 3, "DEX": 14, "CON": 10, "INT": 3, "WIS": 14, "CHA": 6}

**movement:** {"walkFeet": 10, "flyFeet": 30}

**attack:** One melee peck/bite, reach5ft, attack=ownerPB+owner actualWIS modifier, damage1d4piercing; no addedWIS damage and no level scaling.

**sense:** Retain actual sourced keen sight, if present, as advantage on that sense's Perception checks; no automatic darkvision/blindsight.

**limits:** No free Flyby, invisibility, aerial carrying exception, Hover, multiattack or ability to carry a Medium ally. Ordinary falling, weather, carrying and support apply.

### Swimming companion

**id:** feat-animal.swimmer

**abilities:** {"STR": 10, "DEX": 12, "CON": 12, "INT": 3, "WIS": 12, "CHA": 6}

**movement:** {"walkFeet": 5, "swimFeet": 30}

**attack:** One melee bite, reach5ft, attack=ownerPB+owner actualWIS modifier, damage1d6piercing; no addedWIS damage and no level scaling.

**sense:** Retain one actually sourced keen hearing OR smell, if present, for that sense's Perception advantage only.

**limits:** The5ft ground movement is crawling/flopping for a body without legs, paying ordinary crawl cost and suffocation/exposure; no new hands, amphibiousness, Water Breathing, multiattack or sonar.

## NEW feat-only maneuver menu

Martial Adept chooses two of these six; its exact Advanced feat adds two different choices. One shared superiority-die pool and one maneuver per event apply. No current class called Battle Master or its absent menu is presumed.

### Measured Strike

**Activation:** Within one actual weapon attack, before its attack roll. **Cost:** One superiorityd6.

Roll the spentd6 and add it to that one attack roll. Choose before seeing the d20/outcome; natural1 still misses and no extra critical range is created. It adds no damage and supplies no attack, target permission or extra Action.

NEW attack-check enhancement; not an imported Battle Master maneuver.

### Driving Shove

**Activation:** Replace one Attack Action attack with ordinary Shove. **Cost:** One superiorityd6 plus the replaced attack.

Before both opposed rolls, spend/roll thed6 and add it to yourSTR Athletics total for that one ordinary Shove. Target choosesAthletics/Acrobatics; tie preserves state. Actual reach/one-size-larger cap applies; success still pushes5ft away OR Prone, never both solely from this maneuver. It adds no damage.

NEW finite bonus on reviewed ordinary Shove; printed feat/class save-based Shoves are not this maneuver.

### Disarming Feint

**Activation:** Replace one Attack Action attack with ordinary opposed Disarm. **Cost:** One superiorityd6 plus replaced attack.

Choose a reachable actually held droppable item and an ordinary legal Athletics or weapon-check Disarm. Before rolls, spend/roll thed6 and add it to your one opposed total; defender usesAthletics/Acrobatics, tie preserves state. Success uses the existing adjacent/own-space drop placement, not automatic custody. No damage, severed bond or proficiency grant. The save-based Disarm feat and Whip Control are separate procedures and do not acquire this bonus.

NEW finite die on reviewed ordinary Disarm only.

### Guarding Deflection

**Activation:** Reaction when one direct damage event would damage you. **Cost:** One superiorityd6 and the actual Reaction.

After passive defenses but before tempHP, spend/roll1d6 to reduce that whole resolved event by its face, minimum0. Allocate across stable native component order. Attached conditions remain. No re-mitigation of transfer packets that forbid recipient mitigation, HP sacrifice, state assignment or direct death. It neither changes a hit to a miss nor qualifies as Riposte's successful Parry.

NEW finite physical defense using current whole-event/damage-provenance interface.

### Measured Advance

**Activation:** Bonus Action on your own regular turn. **Cost:** One superiorityd6 and the actual BA.

Spend/roll1d6, gaining a separate movement-cost budget of5×face feet resolved immediately. Use one already available movement mode with nonzeroSpeed, pay normal terrain/crawl/squeeze costs, respect legal endpoints and provoke ordinary opportunity attacks. No teleport, new movement mode, free Disengage, replenished ordinary movement or banked distance. Interrupted unspent distance is lost.

NEW bounded movement procedure; no copied Soldier grant.

### Rallying Signal

**Activation:** Action under the ordinary Rally procedure. **Cost:** One superiorityd6 and the actual Action.

Choose one actual ally within30ft able to perceive the rally and one active source-qualifiedFrightened record. Before rolling the ordinaryCHA task check at its sourceDC (otherwise10), spend/roll thed6 and add its face to the total. Apply actual task degrees/natural shift; success ends only that selected record. NoHP/tempHP, loyalty, extra save, new ally turn or automatic cure of other fear records.

NEW finite die on current reviewed Rally.

## Generic Magic Initiate: exact finite curricula

Choose one curriculum, two distinct rank0 spells and one rank1 spell permanently. The rank1 grant has its own1/LR charge under the reproduced casting rules. The abilities below are explicit NEW feat assignments, not changes to ordinary class engines. Fourteen curricula use actual current class rows; Sorcerer and Warlock are finite NEW legacy feat curricula, not new classes. Oracle, Soldier and Warrior have no ordinary low-rank curriculum in the current catalog.

Each choice below preserves its complete original classAccess row and full current procedure card in the structured companion. The authored review index controls current spell status; an older catalog status is retained as history. A pending spell cannot be used through this feat until its own exact payload approval. Generic and named Magic Initiate in the same curriculum belong to one nonduplicating family.
